This continues the E7–8gift visit without a new date/year/grade/age or interval; old origin/December/post-winter-break/adult/bonus clocks stay separate. Sspeech/acts and Mprivate/right/household/inference accounts are not merged. Third piece consumed; shared dinner finished, future cooking declined, escort/thanks/second farewell shown. No fourth piece, accepted future meal, home arrival, new fee or contract settlement. Familiar finite LN01part0012p189–292/all13ruby differs locally; prior189–190exposure retained, no293+orblind score. The separate literal qualification retains WEBp23には versus LNp201に: LN202–204context supports the ignored-request/private-justification reading, but no silentは restoration or proven-typo claim; WEBauthority evidence uses its explicitには.

## KAK-JP-E0013 witness-bounded completed care and unresolved expectation

[E0013 observation index](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0013_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-index) owns 14 disjoint observations over 169 native paragraphs (107 nonblank, 62 LF-only blanks/62 br, no ruby or body media). O###/CHR/REL/PROP/EPI/CLM shorthand means E0013 unless another source is named. The staged cooking-day continuation, compressed later summons and meals, after-WhiteDay report and closing home deposit remain separate clocks; exact date, year, age and grade are unprovided. Sendai is the current focalizer; Miyagi has a new OBSERVED/reported packet while her last own interior, direction and own-readiness remain E10. Local aversion, force and noncompliance, later performed care, completed meal, prospective cooking refusal and private unresolved value remain distinct. Deliberate force does not certify known or intended pain. The ability statement is concessive, and not pressing instruction differs from merely weak desire. No medical efficacy, blanket permission, complete motive or general contract cancellation follows. Familiar finite LN01 part0013 p182–283 has 102 base paragraphs/15 ruby/21 rt and no media; three joins/opening reorder and literal revisions remain source-specific. Novel-only p281 today/summons/same-time wording does not identify the WEB closing visit/note. Active continuous resumption remains current; E14 stays unopened until the whole E13 introducing commit and the prior actual-head green precedes the next major push.

## KAK-JP-E0014 witness-bounded limits, contested gift and academic pressure

[E0014 observation index](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0014_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record). New E14 source-local statements below derive from the complete frozen original245-node encounter and preserved Q1, accepted independent source review and exact prior-lineage readback. Accepted familiar novel correspondence is a separate witness; its added paragraphs, stronger calendar/course wording, image, explicit fee amount and revised material explanation are not WEB events. Independent cumulative component review is complete; transaction and publication credit require their separate whole-unit gates.

Local shorthand is explicitly expanded: O001–015 are KAK-JP-E0014-O001–015 at the actual reading anchor; PROP001–028 are PROP-KAK-JP-E0014-001–028; REL001–015 are REL-KAK-JP-E0014-001–015. CHR001–008 expand to CHR-KAK-JP-E0014-SENDAI_HAZUKI-001–008, CHR009–012 to CHR-KAK-JP-E0014-MIYAGI_SHIORI-009–012. All row IDs below are fully qualified; shorthand never denotes a different unit. Sendai owns the focalized endpoint; new Miyagi state is observed only, with last own interior/direction/readiness at E10. No forecast, model, validated domain or source beyond this unit is added.

[E0015 observation index](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0015_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record). New E15 rows derive from the complete admitted original111-node WEB body and immutable source/Q1, accepted primary/source/finite components and the accepted affected-prior plan. The familiar novel part0014p5–91 is a separate witness: earlier phone timing, contact barrier/no-prior-Sendai-initiation, unrejoined bond, stronger cut-thread and negative-return assertions are not WEB events. Independent cumulative component review is complete; transaction, INDEX and publication gates require their separate actual evidence.

Local shorthand is explicitly expanded: O001–014 mean KAK-JP-E0015-O001–014 in the canonical root partition, not the primary author separate thirteen-row partition; PROP001–024 and REL001–012 mean the fully qualified E0015 IDs printed below. CHR001–008 are CHR-KAK-JP-E0015-MIYAGI_SHIORI-001–008, CHR009–010 are CHR-KAK-JP-E0015-UTSUNOMIYA_MAIKA-009–010. Miyagi has new own interior after E10 and historical E14observed; Sendai own/direction remains E14 by reference, with no current scene or receipt of Miyagi private memory/rationale. All older clocks and models remain.

[E0019 observation index](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0019_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record). This reviewed cumulative unit derives from the complete frozen E19 WEB original and accepted source, primary and affected-prior scope. Independent cumulative review has been completed for this unit; finite correspondence has its separate root acceptance, and no whole transaction, INDEX or publication success is claimed.

OBS-E0019-001–012 are the canonical root partition across159native/100meaningful nodes. The primary author14 groups and other reviewer partitions retain separate attribution. PROP001–028 below means PROP-KAK-JP-E0019; REL001–016 means REL-KAK-JP-E0019. All state and direction endpoints are printed in full.

Sendai own advances E18→E19 and Miyagi observed/voiced E18→E19, while Miyagi own E17 and all later-source own/observed states remain retained. Private anticipated instructions, PE reasoning, feared withdrawal, qualified place/company need, memories, ownership doubts, anger and intent allegations are not Miyagi received knowledge. No new Umina or school scene is created. Actual words plus a head push precede local cessation; anticipated buttons, conditional strike, possible arm loan and later reciprocity are not performed. p102 is tentative formation assessment, p104 visible trace; neither supplies a removal result. Sparse-fridge memory is not current inspection.

Accepted finite A2 is attributed report-only for this author: WEB73 lacks the novel own-home clause, WEB130 reports an alternative rather than the novel prior-speaker conditional question, and WEB152 accuses absent experiment intent rather than novel189 becoming-subject intent. No original current LN collation is claimed here. The private E18 handoff A2 correction occurred after genuine E19 entry and leaves already precise canonical E18 rows exact.

## LN-JP-V10 cumulative reconciliation — 2026-10-24

This appendix adds the complete separately admitted acquired V10 to the same logical instrument. All local O### identifiers resolve to `OBS-LN-JP-V10-O###` in the [V10 observation index](../02%20Sequential%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_LN_V10_DEEP_READING.md#observation-locator-index); native member/p and exact exposure ranges are recoverable there. All66 new propositions and all source-qualified successors are post-exposure literary reconciliation, never a retrospective forecast. Adult own Miyagi ends chapter10; later Miyagi is observed through Sendai's chapter11. The first-HS Sendai and elementary Miyagi packets are separate historical event states disclosed after the adult end. Existing WEB19 own Sendai/observed Miyagi, WEB17 own Miyagi, LN09/BW10 cutoffs, all earlier formulations and model failures remain dated and intact. Static disclosure master remains exact, so its continuation still belongs to one logical disclosure ledger.

Full source text, native fragments, original images and acquisition paths stay private. The frozen I1, BW and LN10 admission inventories are unchanged. Whole-transaction commit earns source coverage; required published-head audit and main integration remain separate. The bounded LN10 source run is complete; external publication verification remains pending until observed, and original I1/E20+/G3–G7 stays paused.

PRESERVE without a new formulation: old actual photo capture/deletion conflicts, private mark-display wishes, all dated local plans, and BW10 start-only observations remain true at their own cutoffs. In particular, PROP-LN-JP-V09-050/058 private love/fear is not turned into a confessed love by new jealousy speech; PROP-LN-JP-V09-028/037 actual image/mark histories are not rewritten by this unperformed display/deletion. WEB PROP-KAK-JP-E0003-012.v2 and E0009-014.v1 retain historical private maternal-departure/anticipated-loss access and unknown Sendai omission reason. The new childhood extra provides reader history, never a retroactive transfer to school-era Sendai or the adult pair. Existing OFF01 private-versus-later-disclosure and OFF02 counterfactual/preparation-not-consumption limits remain explicit in their successors.

## E0020 current source appendix — 2026-10-28

This source-qualified appendix follows the complete frozen E0020 original: 232 native paragraphs, 147 meaningful and 85 format-only; fourteen root OBS groups. Source-first entering freeze, four notes and Q1 precede current comparison and peer findings. It retains the complete original first notes, including the explicitly corrected p116 speaker attribution: Miyagi, identified at p118. These are familiar-source interpretations, not held-out tests. Source/cumulative component review is accepted; whole-transaction and publication acceptance remain distinct, and only the whole introducing commit advances coverage.

Sendai own WEB advances E19→E20; Miyagi observed/voiced advances to E20 while own Miyagi E17 remains exact. Later LN10 adult and historical-extra states stay separate. Private explanation, regret, inferred motive/need, past mental exit option and expected future are not automatic Miyagi knowledge. All old versions and countercases remain unchanged.
